### Task T10b: The privacy test

**Goal:** A test that fails on a private address, a machine identifier, a callsign-shaped token, a VPN or tailnet name, or a hostname that is not on a short allow-list, anywhere in `docs/`, `examples/`, `README.md` or `agents.yaml`.

**Files:**

- Create: `tests/privacy.py`
- Create: `tests/test_privacy.py`

Generic patterns live in the repository: RFC 1918 and link-local addresses, carrier-grade NAT addresses (what a VPN hands out), MAC addresses, `serial: ...` style identifiers, Maidenhead grid squares and callsign-shaped tokens (the placeholders `N0CALL`, `N0TST` and `FN31pr` are allowed), tailnet and zero-trust product names, home-directory paths, and any domain-like token that is not on the allow-list (the one placeholder endpoint host `ollama.lan` is on it). Terms that cannot be written into a public repository at all (an employer's name, regions, real hostnames, serial numbers) cannot be in this test either, so they live in a file **outside** the repository: set `SAFO_PRIVACY_EXTRA_FILE` to its path (one regular expression per line) and the same test applies them. CI runs the generic patterns; the maintainer runs `make check` with the variable set. Nothing in this plan writes those terms down.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the test**

Create `tests/privacy.py`:

```python
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
"""What must never appear in anything public: docs/, examples/ and README.md.

Generic patterns only. Terms that cannot be written into a public repository (an employer's name, a callsign, a
grid square, region names, real hostnames and serials) live in a file OUTSIDE the repository: set
SAFO_PRIVACY_EXTRA_FILE to its path, one regular expression per line, and `make check` applies them too.
CI runs the generic patterns.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import ipaddress
import os
import re
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import urlsplit

ROOT = Path(__file__).parent.parent
SCANNED = ("docs", "examples", "README.md", "agents.yaml")
# Plans embed this test's own fixtures (strings that look private on purpose), so they cannot pass it;
# the specs and every other page do.
EXCLUDED = ("docs/superpowers/plans", "docs/superpowers/specs")
TEXT_SUFFIXES = {".md", ".yaml", ".yml", ".txt", ".json", ".jsonl"}

# A callsign-shaped token: one or two prefix letters, a digit, one to three letters. The placeholders are allowed.
CALLSIGN = r"\b(?:[AKNW][A-Z]?|[A-Z][0-9])[0-9][A-Z]{1,3}\b"
ALLOWED_CALLSIGNS = {"N0CALL", "N0TST", "W3C"}
ALLOWED_GRIDS = {"FN31pr"}

PATTERNS: dict[str, str] = {
    "private IPv4 address": r"\b(?:10\.\d{1,3}|192\.168|172\.(?:1[6-9]|2\d|3[01])|169\.254)\.\d{1,3}\.\d{1,3}\b",
    "carrier-grade NAT address (a VPN's)": r"\b100\.(?:6[4-9]|[7-9]\d|1[01]\d|12[0-7])\.\d{1,3}\.\d{1,3}\b",
    "tailnet name": r"(?i)\b[a-z0-9-]+\.ts\.net\b|\btwingate\b|\btailscale\b",
    "MAC address": r"\b(?:[0-9A-Fa-f]{2}[:-]){5}[0-9A-Fa-f]{2}\b",
    "machine identifier": r"(?i)\b(?:serial(?: number)?|service tag|asset tag)\s*[:#=]\s*[A-Z0-9]{6,}\b",
    "Maidenhead grid square": r"\b[A-R]{2}[0-9]{2}(?:[a-x]{2})?\b",
    "callsign-shaped token": CALLSIGN,
    "home-directory path": r"/home/[a-z][a-z0-9_-]*/",
}

# A domain-like token must be one of these; anything else is a hostname that does not belong in a public document.
HOST = re.compile(r"(?i)\b(?:[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]*\.)+[a-z]{2,63}\b")
# File-like dotted names are not hosts. Single-label private names come from the external supplement.
DOCUMENT_KEYS = {
    "project.number",
    "agents.waiting",
    "agents.working",
    "status.closed",
    "status.merged",
    "status.opened",
    "status.reopened",
    "rules.status.opened",
    "items.totalcount",
}
FILE_SUFFIXES = {"md", "yaml", "yml", "py", "json", "jsonl", "txt", "sh", "toml", "lock"}

ALLOWED_HOSTS = {
    "github.com",
    "api.github.com",
    "docs.github.com",
    "users.noreply.github.com",
    "pypi.org",
    "files.pythonhosted.org",
    "anthropic.com",
    "claude.com",
    "shellcheck.net",
    "ollama.lan",  # the placeholder endpoint host in agents.yaml; real ones live in a git-ignored local file
}


def extra_patterns() -> dict[str, str]:
    path = os.environ.get("SAFO_PRIVACY_EXTRA_FILE", "")
    if not path:
        return {}
    lines = [x for x in Path(path).read_text().splitlines() if x.strip() and not x.startswith("#")]
    return {f"private term {i + 1}": line for i, line in enumerate(lines)}


def scanned_files() -> list[Path]:
    files: list[Path] = []
    for name in SCANNED:
        base = ROOT / name
        if base.is_file():
            files.append(base)
        elif base.is_dir():
            files += [
                p
                for p in sorted(base.rglob("*"))
                if p.is_file() and p.suffix in TEXT_SUFFIXES and not str(p.relative_to(ROOT)).startswith(EXCLUDED)
            ]
    return files


def findings(text: str) -> list[str]:
    """Category and line only: assertion output and repository logs must never repeat private matches."""
    out: list[str] = []

    def record(kind: str, start: int) -> None:
        out.append(f"{kind}: line {text.count(chr(10), 0, start) + 1}")

    for kind, pattern in {**PATTERNS, **extra_patterns()}.items():
        for m in re.finditer(pattern, text):
            token = m.group(0)
            if kind == "callsign-shaped token" and token in ALLOWED_CALLSIGNS:
                continue
            if kind == "Maidenhead grid square" and token in ALLOWED_GRIDS:
                continue
            record(kind, m.start())
    for m in HOST.finditer(text):
        host = m.group(0).lower()
        if host not in ALLOWED_HOSTS and host not in DOCUMENT_KEYS and host.rsplit(".", 1)[-1] not in FILE_SUFFIXES:
            record("hostname", m.start())
    for m in re.finditer(r"https?://[^\s<>\"']+", text, re.I):
        url_host = urlsplit(m.group(0)).hostname
        if url_host and "." not in url_host and ":" not in url_host and url_host.lower() not in ALLOWED_HOSTS:
            record("hostname", m.start())
    for m in re.finditer(r"(?<![\w:])(?:[0-9a-fA-F]*:){2,}[0-9a-fA-F:.]+(?:%[\w-]+)?", text):
        try:
            address = ipaddress.ip_address(m.group(0).split("%", 1)[0])
        except ValueError:
            continue
        if address.is_private or address.is_link_local or address.is_loopback or address.is_unspecified:
            record("private IPv6 address", m.start())
    return out
```

Create `tests/test_privacy.py`:

```python
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
"""Nothing private in anything public: docs/, examples/, README.md."""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from privacy import EXCLUDED, ROOT, findings, scanned_files


@pytest.mark.parametrize("path", scanned_files(), ids=lambda p: str(p.relative_to(ROOT)))
def test_a_public_file_names_no_host_machine_or_callsign(path: Path) -> None:
    hits = findings(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert not hits, f"{path.relative_to(ROOT)}: " + "; ".join(hits)


def test_only_the_plans_and_specs_are_excluded() -> None:
    assert EXCLUDED == ("docs/superpowers/plans", "docs/superpowers/specs")


def test_the_scan_covers_the_docs_the_examples_and_the_readme() -> None:
    names = {str(p.relative_to(ROOT)) for p in scanned_files()}
    assert {"README.md", "agents.yaml", "examples/renegade-penguin.yaml", "examples/outcomes-2026-10.jsonl"} <= names


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "text,category",
    [
        ("the box at 192.168.1.20 and 10.0.0.5", "private IPv4 address"),
        ("NAS at storage-01.local", "hostname"),
        ("ssh build.lan", "hostname"),
        ("serial: ABC123XYZ9", "machine identifier"),
        ("asset tag # 0042A9", "machine identifier"),
        ("de K1ABC listening", "callsign-shaped token"),
        ("grid FN42ab", "Maidenhead grid square"),
        ("aa:bb:cc:dd:ee:ff", "MAC address"),
        ("/home/someone/src", "home-directory path"),
        ("see intranet.example.corp", "hostname"),
        ("the box at 172.20.4.9 and 169.254.1.1", "private IPv4 address"),
        ("reach it at 100.101.102.103 over the VPN", "carrier-grade NAT address (a VPN's)"),
        ("host mybox.tail1234.ts.net", "tailnet name"),
        ("through twingate or Tailscale", "tailnet name"),
        ("http://nas.home:11434", "hostname"),
    ],
)
def test_the_patterns_catch_what_they_are_for(text: str, category: str) -> None:
    assert any(hit.startswith(category + ":") for hit in findings(text))


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "text",
    [
        "N0CALL and N0TST are the placeholders",
        "grid FN31pr",
        "https://github.com/ChiefGyk3D/scrum-around-and-find-out",
        "api.github.com:443 pypi.org:443 files.pythonhosted.org:443",
        "http://ollama.lan:11434 and http://ollama.lan:11435",
        "a zero-trust remote-access client",
        "agents.local.yaml and ~/.config/safo/agents.local.yaml",
        "board.yaml, action.yml, requirements.txt, P2 later, S (hours), M (a day)",
        "Python 3.11, the W3C, PyYAML 6.0.3 and scripts/apply-baseline.sh",
    ],
)
def test_ordinary_text_passes(text: str) -> None:
    assert findings(text) == []


def test_private_terms_come_from_a_file_outside_the_repository(tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
    extra = tmp_path / "private.txt"
    extra.write_text("# one regex per line\nacme-secret-corp\n")
    monkeypatch.setenv("SAFO_PRIVACY_EXTRA_FILE", str(extra))
    assert findings("worked at acme-secret-corp once") == ["private term 1: line 1"]
    assert findings("nothing here") == []


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "text,category",
    [
        ("https://private.example.xyz/path", "hostname"),
        ("HTTPS://PRIVATE.EXAMPLE.XYZ/path", "hostname"),
        ("fd00::1", "private IPv6 address"),
        ("http://singleprivate/path", "hostname"),
    ],
)
def test_domain_and_ipv6_bypasses_are_detected(text: str, category: str) -> None:
    assert any(hit.startswith(category + ":") for hit in findings(text))


def test_failure_diagnostics_never_repeat_private_terms(tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
    import traceback

    extra = tmp_path / "private.txt"
    extra.write_text("sentinel-private-term\n")
    monkeypatch.setenv("SAFO_PRIVACY_EXTRA_FILE", str(extra))
    hits = findings("sentinel-private-term")
    assert hits
    try:
        assert not hits, "public.md: " + "; ".join(hits)
    except AssertionError as err:
        diagnostic = "".join(traceback.format_exception_only(err))
    assert "sentinel-private-term" not in diagnostic
```

### Task T10c: Wiki publishing through GYST's existing wiki workflow

**Goal:** The wiki is a generated mirror of `docs/`, published by GYST's `wiki-publish.yml` (the same reusable workflow other repositories call), with a small generator and a test that keeps the two honest.

**Files:**

- Create: `tests/test_wiki.py`
- Create: `scripts/gen_wiki.py`
- Create: `.github/workflows/wiki.yml`

GYST already has `wiki.yml` (its own caller) and `wiki-publish.yml` (the reusable workflow); no new GYST workflow is needed. A caller supplies a `generate-command` that writes the page tree into `$OUT_DIR`; the reusable workflow generates on every run (so a broken generator fails review) and publishes only from the default branch. `scripts/gen_wiki.py` is standard library only: each `docs/<name>.md` becomes the page `<Name>`; a relative link to another page becomes the wiki name; a relative link to anything else in the repository becomes a link to that file on `main`; a link to nothing, a page the sidebar does not list, or an em dash is an error.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `tests/test_wiki.py`:

````python
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
"""scripts/gen_wiki.py: the wiki is generated from docs/, links are rewritten, mistakes are errors."""

from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.util
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from types import ModuleType

import pytest

ROOT = Path(__file__).parent.parent
SCRIPT = ROOT / "scripts" / "gen_wiki.py"


def load() -> ModuleType:
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("gen_wiki", SCRIPT)
    assert spec and spec.loader
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    sys.modules["gen_wiki"] = module
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


gen = load()


def test_every_doc_page_becomes_a_wiki_page_with_a_sidebar_and_a_footer() -> None:
    tree = gen.generate("abc1234")
    assert {"Home.md", "Why.md", "Lessons.md", "Board-yaml.md", "Agents-status.md", "_Sidebar.md", "_Footer.md"} <= set(
        tree
    )
    assert "abc1234" in tree["_Footer.md"]
    assert "[Agents status](Agents-status)" in tree["_Sidebar.md"]


def test_links_between_pages_become_wiki_names_and_other_links_point_at_main() -> None:
    tree = gen.generate("x")
    assert "[Lessons](Lessons)" in tree["Limits.md"]
    assert (
        "(https://github.com/ChiefGyk3D/scrum-around-and-find-out/blob/main/examples/renegade-penguin.yaml)"
        in tree["Adoption.md"]
    )


def test_a_link_inside_a_code_fence_is_left_alone() -> None:
    page = ROOT / "docs" / "adoption.md"
    text = "```md\n[x](nowhere.md)\n```\n[y](why.md)\n"
    assert gen.rewrite_links(text, page) == "```md\n[x](nowhere.md)\n```\n[y](Why)\n"


def test_a_link_to_nothing_is_an_error() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(gen.WikiError, match="points at nothing"):
        gen.rewrite_links("[x](missing.md)", ROOT / "docs" / "why.md")


def test_a_page_the_sidebar_does_not_list_is_an_error(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setattr(gen, "ORDER", [n for n in gen.ORDER if n != "limits"])
    with pytest.raises(gen.WikiError, match="does not list"):
        gen.sources()


def test_check_mode_compares_an_existing_tree(tmp_path: Path, capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str]) -> None:
    assert gen.main(["--out", str(tmp_path), "--commit", "abc"]) == 0
    assert gen.main(["--check", "--out", str(tmp_path), "--commit", "abc"]) == 0
    (tmp_path / "Why.md").write_text("changed\n")
    assert gen.main(["--check", "--out", str(tmp_path), "--commit", "abc"]) == 1
    assert "stale: Why.md" in capsys.readouterr().err


def test_a_stale_page_is_removed_on_the_next_write(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    (tmp_path / "Old.md").write_text("gone\n")
    gen.main(["--out", str(tmp_path), "--commit", "abc"])
    assert not (tmp_path / "Old.md").exists()


def test_no_page_uses_an_em_dash_and_every_page_opens_with_a_title() -> None:
    for path in (ROOT / "docs").glob("*.md"):
        text = path.read_text()
        assert text.startswith("# "), path.name
        assert "—" not in text, path.name


def test_the_lessons_are_dated_and_carry_the_seeded_entries() -> None:
    text = (ROOT / "docs" / "lessons.md").read_text()
    for needle in (
        "updateProjectV2Field",
        "$endCursor",
        "UI-only",
        "createProjectV2View",
        "personal repository",
        "totalCount",
        "background jobs",
        "29%",
        "worktrees",
        "mergeable",
        "bypassForcePushAllowances",
        "git show",
    ):
        assert needle in text, needle
    assert text.count("**What happened.**") == text.count("**Evidence.**") == text.count("**Rule now.**") >= 12


@pytest.mark.parametrize("kind", ["missing", "file"])
def test_check_explicit_comparison_directory_must_exist(tmp_path: Path, kind: str) -> None:
    target = tmp_path / "compare"
    if kind == "file":
        target.write_text("not a directory")
    assert gen.main(["--check", "--out", str(target)]) == 1


@pytest.mark.parametrize("text,error", [("No title\n", "must open"), ("# Title\n\nText — text\n", "em dash")])
def test_generate_rejects_malformed_temporary_pages(
    tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, text: str, error: str
) -> None:
    page = tmp_path / "page.md"
    page.write_text(text)
    monkeypatch.setattr(gen, "ROOT", tmp_path)
    monkeypatch.setattr(gen, "sources", lambda: {"page": page})
    with pytest.raises(gen.WikiError, match=error):
        gen.generate("test")
````

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the generator and the caller workflow**

Create `scripts/gen_wiki.py`:

````python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
"""Generate the GitHub wiki from docs/.

The wiki is a mirror, never a place to write: `.github/workflows/wiki.yml` calls GYST's wiki-publish.yml, which
replaces every page with this output on each push to main. Standard library only.

    scripts/gen_wiki.py --out DIR [--commit SHA]     write the wiki tree
    scripts/gen_wiki.py --check [--out DIR]          validate; compare DIR when it exists

A page is `docs/<name>.md`, published as `<Name>` (first letter upper-cased). `ORDER` is the sidebar; a page not
in it, or an entry with no page, is an error. A relative link to another page becomes the wiki page; a relative
link to anything else in the repository becomes a link to that file on main; a link to nothing is an error.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import posixpath
import re
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
DOCS = ROOT / "docs"
REPO_URL = "https://github.com/ChiefGyk3D/scrum-around-and-find-out"
ORDER = [
    "home",
    "why",
    "roles",
    "routing",
    "usage",
    "handoff",
    "limits",
    "board",
    "board-yaml",
    "agents-status",
    "lessons",
    "adoption",
]
INLINE = re.compile(r"(\]\()([^)\s]+)((?:\s+\"[^\"]*\")?\))")
FENCE = re.compile(r"^\s*(```|~~~)")
EXTERNAL = re.compile(r"^(?:[a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*:|//|#|/)", re.IGNORECASE)


class WikiError(Exception):
    """A tree the wiki cannot be generated from."""


def wiki_name(stem: str) -> str:
    return stem[:1].upper() + stem[1:]


def sources() -> dict[str, Path]:
    found = {p.stem: p for p in sorted(DOCS.glob("*.md"))}
    missing = [n for n in ORDER if n not in found]
    extra = [n for n in found if n not in ORDER]
    if missing:
        raise WikiError(f"ORDER names pages with no docs/<name>.md: {', '.join(missing)}")
    if extra:
        raise WikiError(f"docs/ has pages the sidebar does not list (add them to ORDER): {', '.join(extra)}")
    return found


def rewrite_target(target: str, page: Path) -> str:
    if EXTERNAL.match(target):
        return target
    path, _, anchor = target.partition("#")
    resolved = Path(posixpath.normpath(posixpath.join(page.parent.as_posix(), path)))
    if not resolved.exists():
        raise WikiError(f"{page.relative_to(ROOT)}: link to {target!r} points at nothing")
    suffix = f"#{anchor}" if anchor else ""
    if resolved.parent == DOCS and resolved.suffix == ".md":
        return wiki_name(resolved.stem) + suffix
    relative = resolved.resolve().relative_to(ROOT).as_posix()
    kind = "tree" if resolved.is_dir() else "blob"
    return f"{REPO_URL}/{kind}/main/{relative}{suffix}"


def rewrite_links(text: str, page: Path) -> str:
    out: list[str] = []
    in_fence = False
    for line in text.splitlines():
        if FENCE.match(line):
            in_fence = not in_fence
        elif not in_fence:
            line = INLINE.sub(lambda m: f"{m[1]}{rewrite_target(m[2], page)}{m[3]}", line)
        out.append(line)
    return "\n".join(out) + "\n"


def sidebar() -> str:
    lines = ["**Scrum Around and Find Out**", ""]
    for stem in ORDER:
        title = "Home" if stem == "home" else wiki_name(stem).replace("-", " ")
        lines.append(f"- [{title}]({wiki_name(stem)})")
    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n"


def generate(commit: str) -> dict[str, str]:
    tree: dict[str, str] = {}
    for stem, path in sources().items():
        text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        if not text.startswith("# "):
            raise WikiError(f"{path.relative_to(ROOT)} must open with a '# Title' line")
        if "—" in text:
            raise WikiError(f"{path.relative_to(ROOT)} contains an em dash; use a comma, a colon or two sentences")
        tree[f"{wiki_name(stem)}.md"] = rewrite_links(text, path)
    tree["_Sidebar.md"] = sidebar()
    tree["_Footer.md"] = f"Generated from `docs/` at {commit}. Do not edit here: edit the files in the repository.\n"
    return tree


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    parser.add_argument("--out", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--commit", default="HEAD")
    parser.add_argument("--check", action="store_true")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    try:
        tree = generate(args.commit)
    except WikiError as err:
        print(f"error: {err}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    if args.check:
        if args.out is not None and not args.out.is_dir():
            print("error: comparison directory missing or not a directory", file=sys.stderr)
            return 1
        if args.out is not None:
            stale = [n for n, text in tree.items() if not (args.out / n).exists() or (args.out / n).read_text() != text]
            stale += [p.name for p in args.out.glob("*.md") if p.name not in tree]
            if stale:
                print(f"error: {args.out} is stale: {', '.join(sorted(set(stale)))}", file=sys.stderr)
                return 1
        print(f"ok: {len(tree)} wiki files")
        return 0
    if not args.out:
        parser.error("--out DIR is required unless --check")
    args.out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    for old in args.out.glob("*.md"):
        old.unlink()
    for name, text in tree.items():
        (args.out / name).write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"wrote {len(tree)} wiki files to {args.out}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
````

Create `.github/workflows/wiki.yml`:

```yaml
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
# Publishes this repository's GitHub wiki from docs/ on every push to main, with GYST's wiki-publish.yml.
# The wiki is a generated mirror (scripts/gen_wiki.py): each publish replaces every page, so edit docs/, never the wiki.
#
# One thing the repository owner does once, and GitHub offers only in the UI: a wiki's git remote does not exist until
# its first page is made. Open the Wiki tab, choose "Create the first page", save anything. Until then the publish job
# fails with a message naming that step, and nothing else in CI is affected.
name: Wiki

on:
  push:
    branches: [main]
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  wiki:
    uses: ChiefGyk3D/git-your-ship-together/.github/workflows/wiki-publish.yml@1360d100a45897b2d658272441bc5238d6532743 # v1.16.0
    permissions:
      contents: write   # the publish job only; the generate job holds contents: read
    with:
      publish: true
      generate-command: python scripts/gen_wiki.py --out "$OUT_DIR" --commit "$SOURCE_SHA"
      egress-policy: block
```

**MAINTAINER STEP (once, in the UI).** GitHub creates a wiki's git repository only when its first page is made: open the repository's Wiki tab, choose "Create the first page", save anything. Until then the `wiki / publish` job fails with a message naming this step and nothing else is affected. The first publish replaces every page.

Create `tests/test_examples.py`:

```python
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
"""The project's own board and its scheduled reconcile: user-owned, no token in CI, run from the maintainer's login."""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

from safo.schema import load_board

EXAMPLES = Path(__file__).parent.parent / "examples"


def test_the_projects_own_board_is_user_owned_and_has_no_number_until_bootstrap_creates_it() -> None:
    board = load_board(EXAMPLES / "safo.yaml")
    assert board.project.owner_type == "user" and board.project.number is None
    assert [r.full_name for r in board.repositories] == ["ChiefGyk3D/scrum-around-and-find-out"]


def test_the_timer_runs_reconcile_from_the_users_own_login_with_no_token_stored() -> None:
    service = (EXAMPLES / "systemd" / "safo-reconcile.service").read_text()
    exec_line = next(line for line in service.splitlines() if line.startswith("ExecStart="))
    assert exec_line.endswith(".config/safo/board.yaml reconcile")
    assert not any(line.startswith(("Environment", "EnvironmentFile")) for line in service.splitlines())
    assert "SAFO_TOKEN" not in service
    assert "OnCalendar=daily" in (EXAMPLES / "systemd" / "safo-reconcile.timer").read_text()


def test_no_workflow_gives_the_own_board_a_personal_access_token() -> None:
    for path in (EXAMPLES.parent / ".github" / "workflows").glob("*.yml"):
        text = path.read_text()
        assert "allow-token-for-org" not in text and "inputs.token" not in text
        assert "secrets.GH_PAT" not in text and "PERSONAL_ACCESS_TOKEN" not in text


def test_own_board_number_belongs_in_local_copy_and_service_reads_that_copy(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    import yaml

    source = EXAMPLES / "safo.yaml"
    before = source.read_text()
    local = tmp_path / "board.yaml"
    data = yaml.safe_load(before)
    data["project"]["number"] = 1
    local.write_text(yaml.safe_dump(data))
    assert load_board(local).project.number == 1 and source.read_text() == before
    service = (EXAMPLES / "systemd" / "safo-reconcile.service").read_text()
    assert "%h/.config/safo/board.yaml reconcile" in service
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the own-board example and the scheduled reconcile**

Create `examples/safo.yaml`:

```yaml
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
# This project's own board: a user-owned GitHub Project, created by `safo bootstrap` (the first real use) and
# kept current by a scheduled `safo reconcile` run from the maintainer's own gh login (examples/systemd/).
# No App can write a user-owned board and no token is kept in CI.
#
# Copy this immutable example to your local board file; write the discovered project number in that copy.
version: 1
project:
  owner: ChiefGyk3D
  owner_type: user
  title: Scrum Around and Find Out
repositories:
  - owner: ChiefGyk3D
    name: scrum-around-and-find-out
    default_area: Core
    area_rules:
      - {match: action, area: Action}
      - {match: docs, area: Docs}
      - {match: wiki, area: Docs}
      - {match: route, area: Routing}
      - {match: usage, area: Routing}
      - {match: ollama, area: Routing}
      - {match: ci, area: CI and release}
      - {match: release, area: CI and release}
fields:
  - name: Status
    type: single_select
    options:
      - {name: Backlog, color: GRAY, description: "Not scheduled"}
      - {name: Next, color: BLUE, description: "Scheduled for the current or next sprint"}
      - {name: In progress, color: YELLOW, description: ""}
      - {name: Blocked, color: RED, description: "Needs the maintainer"}
      - {name: Done, color: GREEN, description: ""}
  - name: Area
    type: single_select
    options:
      - {name: Core, color: BLUE, description: "Client, schema, modes"}
      - {name: Action, color: PURPLE, description: "action.yml and credentials"}
      - {name: Routing, color: ORANGE, description: "agents.yaml, usage, route, outcomes, local LLM"}
      - {name: Docs, color: GRAY, description: "docs/ and the wiki"}
      - {name: CI and release, color: YELLOW, description: "Workflows, baseline, releases"}
  - name: Priority
    type: single_select
    options:
      - {name: P1 soon, color: ORANGE, description: ""}
      - {name: P2 later, color: YELLOW, description: ""}
      - {name: P3 someday, color: GRAY, description: ""}
  - name: Agent
    type: single_select
    options:
      - {name: Claude, color: ORANGE, description: "Claude lead or a Claude subagent"}
      - {name: Codex, color: GREEN, description: "OpenAI Codex"}
      - {name: Copilot, color: BLUE, description: "GitHub Copilot coding agent"}
      - {name: You, color: PURPLE, description: "The maintainer"}
  - {name: Sprint, type: iteration, start: 2026-10-20, duration_days: 14, count: 6}
  - {name: Done on, type: date}
views:
  - {name: Board, layout: board, filter: "-status:Done"}
  - {name: Agents at work, layout: table, filter: 'status:"In progress",Next'}
rules:
  status:
    opened: Backlog
    opened_pr: In progress
    draft_pr: Backlog
    reopened: Backlog
    closed: Done
    merged: Done
  done_date_field: Done on
  new_item_defaults:
    Priority: P2 later
agents:
  field: Agent
  working: [In progress, Next]
  waiting: [Blocked]
ui_only:
  - "Board view: Group by = Status"
  - "Workflows: Item closed -> Done; Pull request merged -> Done"
```

Create `examples/systemd/safo-reconcile.service`:

```text
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
# Keeps a user-owned board current from your own gh login. Install under ~/.config/systemd/user/ and edit the paths:
#   systemctl --user daemon-reload && systemctl --user enable --now safo-reconcile.timer
# No token is stored: safo asks `gh auth token` each run. Check the active account first (`gh auth status`).
[Unit]
Description=Keep the SAFO project board current (safo reconcile)
Wants=network-online.target
After=network-online.target

[Service]
Type=oneshot
ExecStart=%h/.local/bin/safo --board %h/.config/safo/board.yaml reconcile
```

Create `examples/systemd/safo-reconcile.timer`:

```text
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
[Unit]
Description=Run safo reconcile every day

[Timer]
OnCalendar=daily
RandomizedDelaySec=15m
Persistent=true

[Install]
WantedBy=timers.target
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the on-demand live audit**

`workflow_dispatch` only: it needs the App's key, and a pull request must never receive a secret. The key comes from Doppler over this job's OIDC identity exactly as GYST's `project-sync.yml` fetches it (no personal access token). `tests/test_workflows.py` already has the rule that this file may only trigger on `workflow_dispatch`; it was skipped until now.

Create `.github/workflows/live-audit.yml`:

```yaml
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
# One live, read-only `safo audit` of the Renegade-Penguin suite board against examples/renegade-penguin.yaml.
# Run by hand (Actions, Live audit, Run workflow). Never on a pull request: it needs the App's key, and a
# pull request must never receive a secret. Exit 0 means the example still matches the live board.
#
# The key is fetched from Doppler over this job's OIDC identity, as GYST's project-sync.yml does; no personal
# access token. The owner sets this up once (docs/adoption.md): the repository variables PROJECTS_APP_CLIENT_ID
# and PROJECTS_DOPPLER_IDENTITY_ID, and this repository's subject on that identity.
name: Live audit

on:
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  audit:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 10
    permissions:
      contents: read
      id-token: write   # the Doppler OIDC fetch of the App key
    steps:
      - uses: step-security/harden-runner@e14015d583714f6e62063499dc959a02595150a1 # v2.21.1
        with:
          egress-policy: block
          allowed-endpoints: >-
            api.doppler.com:443 api.github.com:443 github.com:443 pypi.org:443 files.pythonhosted.org:443
          disable-sudo: true
      - uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1
        with:
          persist-credentials: false
      - name: Fetch the App key from Doppler (OIDC)
        uses: dopplerhq/secrets-fetch-action@451892f16195f9ac360e1a5bcbf0b5fd0e957534 # v2.0.0
        with:
          auth-method: oidc
          doppler-identity-id: ${{ vars.PROJECTS_DOPPLER_IDENTITY_ID }}
          doppler-project: projects
          doppler-config: prd
          inject-env-vars: true
      - name: Audit the live board
        uses: ./ # zizmor: ignore[self-repository]
        with:
          mode: audit
          board-file: examples/renegade-penguin.yaml
          client-id: ${{ vars.PROJECTS_APP_CLIENT_ID }}
          private-key: ${{ env.PROJECTS_APP_PRIVATE_KEY }}
          app-owner: Renegade-Penguin
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Audit the real suite board (read-only)**

First confirm which `gh` account is active: `gh auth status` (this must be the account that can read the Renegade-Penguin project; it needs the `project` scope, `gh auth refresh -s project`). `audit` sends queries only.

```bash
.venv/bin/safo --board examples/renegade-penguin.yaml audit; echo "exit=$?"
```

Expected (first run): some `DRIFT` and `NOTE` lines, then `exit=1`. Colour and description differences are `NOTE` lines.

Read each line and fix `examples/renegade-penguin.yaml`, never the live board:

| Line | Meaning | Fix in the example |
|---|---|---|
| `DRIFT field 'X' is missing from the project` | The example names a field the board lacks | Remove it from the example (or ask the maintainer to add it in the UI) |
| `DRIFT field 'X' lacks the option 'Y'` | The example lists an option the board does not have | Remove the option |
| `DRIFT field 'X' has the option 'Y', which board.yaml does not list` | The board has an option the example omits | Add it |
| `DRIFT field 'Sprint' lacks the iterations starting ...` | `count` is higher than the live iteration list | Lower `count` |
| `DRIFT view 'V' ...` | A view differs or is missing | Match its layout and filter, or remove it |
| `NOTE ... live colour/description ...` | Same name, other colour | Copy the live colour and description into the example |
| `DRIFT ADD ...`, `DONE ...`, `DATE ...`, `AREA ...` | A card the board does not have yet | Not an example problem: the board is behind. Report it; do not run `reconcile` live without the maintainer |
| `UNKNOWN repository X cannot be read` | The token's account has no access to that repository | Note it for the maintainer |

```bash
.venv/bin/safo --board examples/renegade-penguin.yaml audit; echo "exit=$?"
```

Expected after the example is fixed: no `DRIFT` line about fields, options, iterations or views, and any remaining lines are board-behind `ADD`/`DONE` items the maintainer decides about. `exit=0` once the board is also current.

- [ ] **Step 7: Prove bootstrap and reconcile are safe against the real board (dry run, read-only)**

```bash
.venv/bin/safo --dry-run --board examples/renegade-penguin.yaml bootstrap; echo "exit=$?"
.venv/bin/safo --dry-run --board examples/renegade-penguin.yaml reconcile; echo "exit=$?"
```

Expected: bootstrap prints `nothing to create: the project already has everything board.yaml names` and `exit=0`; reconcile prints the changes it would make as `dry run: would ...` lines and sends none.

- [ ] **Step 8: Commit and open the seventh pull request**

```bash
git add examples/renegade-penguin.yaml tests/test_examples.py examples/safo.yaml examples/systemd/safo-reconcile.service examples/systemd/safo-reconcile.timer .github/workflows/live-audit.yml
git diff --cached --name-only -- examples/renegade-penguin.yaml | .venv/bin/python -c 'import sys; assert sys.stdin.read().strip() == "examples/renegade-penguin.yaml"'
git commit -m "T11: own-board example and timer, the on-demand live audit, and the suite example fixed against the live board" -m "The own board is user-owned and reconciled from the maintainer's login; live-audit.yml is workflow_dispatch only." -m "Co-Authored-By: Claude <noreply@anthropic.com>"
```

```bash
git push -u origin build/dogfood
gh pr create -R ChiefGyk3D/scrum-around-and-find-out --title "Dogfood: live audit, own board" --body "T11 of the v0.1.0 plan. Audit output attached as a comment."
```

Expected: a pull request URL; CI green.

**MAINTAINER STEPS (these write to GitHub; the implementer prepares and does not run them).**

1. Copy `examples/safo.yaml` to `$HOME/.config/safo/board.yaml` (create its directory first). Bootstrap that local copy, write the discovered `number: N` in that copy, do its UI checklist, and audit the local copy until `clean`. The tracked example stays numberless.
2. Fill it: `safo --board "$HOME/.config/safo/board.yaml" --dry-run reconcile`, read it, then `safo --board ${HOME}/.config/safo/board.yaml reconcile`; run it again and expect `nothing to do`.
3. Install the timer from `examples/systemd/` (edit the paths in the service) and `systemctl --user enable --now safo-reconcile.timer`. The reconcile runs from your own `gh` login; no token is stored anywhere.
4. For the on-demand live audit in CI: add this repository to the Doppler identity's allowed subjects for `projects`/`prd` (the same identity GYST's project-sync uses), set the repository variables `PROJECTS_APP_CLIENT_ID` and `PROJECTS_DOPPLER_IDENTITY_ID`, and run Actions, Live audit.
5. Two weekly reconciles of the suite board are checked by hand before the dotfiles board tools retire (spec, migration step 3).

### Task T12: GYST hand-off and the v0.1.0 release

**Goal:** Turn GYST's `project-sync.yml` into a thin wrapper that calls this Action at a pinned commit (inputs unchanged for callers), then cut and sign `v0.1.0`. This task is a plan for work in another repository, validated against a scratch copy of GYST `main` (its whole test suite passes with these changes); each step is marked as the implementer's or the maintainer's.

**Order matters.** The wrapper pins a SAFO commit, so SAFO's `v0.1.0` must exist first. Do the release (part A), then the GYST pull request (part B), then the callers' pin bumps (part C).

#### Part A: release v0.1.0 (this repository)

- [ ] **Step 1: Preconditions (implementer checks, maintainer decides)**

All of: PRs 1 to 7 merged; `main` green; the live audit run by hand reports no structural drift; `safo --dry-run bootstrap` against the suite board prints `nothing to create`; `safo reconcile` has run against the suite board once with the maintainer watching and a second run printed `nothing to do`; the own board exists. The spec says v0.1.0 is cut when `bootstrap`, `audit` and `reconcile` are proven against the real suite board.

- [ ] **Step 2: Check the version**

`src/safo/__init__.py` already says `0.1.0`, and `release.yml`'s `verify-command` refuses a tag that disagrees with it.

```bash
git switch main && git pull --ff-only
grep -n '__version__' src/safo/__init__.py
git fetch --tags && git tag -l | sort -V | tail -n 3
```

Expected: `__version__ = "0.1.0"`, and no `v0.1.0` tag yet.

- [ ] **Step 3: MAINTAINER: sign and push the tag**

Fetch tags first and look at what exists before choosing a number (GYST's own rule; a tag cut from a stale view once had to be re-cut). The tag ruleset made by `apply-baseline.sh` makes `v*` tags immutable, so a mistaken tag is superseded by the next number, never moved.

```bash
git tag -s v0.1.0 -m "v0.1.0: board as code, the Action, agents-status and agent routing"
git verify-tag v0.1.0
git push origin v0.1.0
```

Expected: `Good signature`, then the push. The `Release` workflow (GYST's `python-package-release.yml`, `pypi: false`) builds the sdist and wheel, checks the tag against `__version__`, records build provenance and attaches the files to a GitHub release.

- [ ] **Step 4: Record the commit the callers will pin**

```bash
git verify-tag v0.1.0
safo_release_commit=$(git rev-parse 'v0.1.0^{commit}')
git show "${safo_release_commit}:src/safo/__init__.py" | python -c 'import ast, sys; tree = ast.parse(sys.stdin.read()); assert any(isinstance(n, ast.Assign) and any(isinstance(t, ast.Name) and t.id == "__version__" for t in n.targets) and isinstance(n.value, ast.Constant) and n.value.value == "0.1.0" for n in tree.body)'
export SAFO_RELEASE_COMMIT="$safo_release_commit"
```

Expected: a valid signed SAFO tag and version check, then a 40-character resolved commit in `SAFO_RELEASE_COMMIT`. Keep the same shell when changing repositories; export it again from the verified tag if starting a new shell. This verified value is `<safo-sha>` below. Run this in the SAFO checkout before moving to GYST.

#### Part B: the GYST pull request (a separate repository; the maintainer merges)

- [ ] **Step 5: Work in a worktree, never in the shared checkout**

A shared checkout moved to another ref to read one file is how a detached HEAD was left behind once. Use `git show origin/main:path` to read, and a worktree to write.

```bash
cd /home/chiefgyk3d/src/git-your-ship-together
git fetch origin
git worktree add ../gyst-project-sync-wrapper -b project-sync-wrapper origin/main
cd ../gyst-project-sync-wrapper
```

(Not run by the implementer; see the note.)

- [ ] **Step 6: Edit `.github/workflows/project-sync.yml`**

Everything above `jobs:` keeps its inputs exactly; three things change.

1. Replace the two comment paragraphs that describe the inlined script and the token mint (from `# The script is scripts/project_sync.py ...` through `access token is accepted.`) with:

```yaml
# The sync itself is ChiefGyk3D/scrum-around-and-find-out (safo), called below by
# commit with a `# vX.Y.Z` comment. This file is the delivery layer: the inputs
# callers already pass, the Doppler fetch of the App key over this job's OIDC
# identity, and harden-runner. safo mints the one-hour installation token from
# that key (actions/create-github-app-token, limited to the calling
# repository's installation and revoked when the job ends), reads the event
# payload as data and never the pull request's title, and talks to
# api.github.com. No personal access token is accepted.
#
```

2. In the `allowed-endpoints` input, add what safo's install needs under `block`: `default: "api.doppler.com:443 api.github.com:443 files.pythonhosted.org:443 pypi.org:443"`. Run the CI fixture once in `audit` egress mode and read the egress report before trusting this list; add `github.com:443` if the report shows setup-python downloading Python.

3. Replace everything from the comment `# The key reaches the action as an input` to the end of the file (the "Require an App identifier" step, both token-mint steps and the inlined script) with:

```yaml
      # The key reaches safo as an input, never a command line; with no key (a
      # dry run with no Doppler) nothing is minted and safo says so and succeeds.
      # `reconcile: false` makes the schedule and manual runs a no-op, as before.
      - name: Sync the project
        if: ${{ inputs.reconcile || (github.event_name != 'schedule' && github.event_name != 'workflow_dispatch') }}
        uses: ChiefGyk3D/scrum-around-and-find-out@<safo-sha> # v0.1.0
        with:
          mode: ${{ inputs.dry-run && env.PROJECTS_APP_PRIVATE_KEY == '' && 'validate' || (github.event_name == 'schedule' || github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch') && 'reconcile' || 'sync' }}
          project-url: ${{ inputs.project-url }}
          status-field: ${{ inputs.status-field }}
          status-open-issue: ${{ inputs.status-open-issue }}
          status-open-pr: ${{ inputs.status-open-pr }}
          status-draft-pr: ${{ inputs.status-draft-pr }}
          status-done: ${{ inputs.status-done }}
          done-date-field: ${{ inputs.done-date-field }}
          default-area-field: ${{ inputs.default-area-field }}
          default-area: ${{ inputs.default-area }}
          dry-run: ${{ inputs.dry-run }}
          client-id: ${{ inputs.client-id }}
          app-id: ${{ inputs.app-id }}
          private-key: ${{ env.PROJECTS_APP_PRIVATE_KEY }}
          repositories: ${{ github.event.repository.name }}
```

Replace `<safo-sha>` with the verified SHA from Part A and create the wrapper test's record, in the GYST worktree:

```bash
python - <<'PIN'
import re
from pathlib import Path

import os

sha = os.environ["SAFO_RELEASE_COMMIT"]
assert re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{40}", sha)
assert sha != "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef01234567"
workflow = Path(".github/workflows/project-sync.yml")
text = workflow.read_text()
assert "<safo-sha>" in text
workflow.write_text(text.replace("<safo-sha>", sha))
Path("tests/safo-release-commit.txt").write_text(sha + "\n")
PIN
```

The wrapper test compares the pin to the signed-tag/version-verified record. A dummy or unresolved placeholder fails. The first harden-runner step and all Doppler steps stay as they are.

- [ ] **Step 7: Delete the script, its inliner and its tests; add the wrapper's contract test**

```bash
git rm scripts/project_sync.py scripts/inline_project_sync.py tests/test_project_sync.py
```

(Not run by the implementer; see the note.)

Create `tests/test_project_sync_wrapper.py`:

```python
"""project-sync.yml is a thin wrapper around safo: the inputs callers pass, the pin and the plumbing.

The sync logic lives in ChiefGyk3D/scrum-around-and-find-out and is tested there. What stays here is the
contract with callers: every input they pass today is still accepted, the Action is called by commit with a
version comment, the App key still arrives from Doppler over OIDC, and nothing but safo mints a token or
holds a script.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from pathlib import Path

import yaml

REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
WORKFLOW = REPO / ".github" / "workflows" / "project-sync.yml"

# The inputs callers pass today. Removing or renaming one breaks a caller; adding one is fine.
CALLER_INPUTS = {
    "project-url",
    "status-field",
    "status-open-issue",
    "status-open-pr",
    "status-draft-pr",
    "status-done",
    "done-date-field",
    "reconcile",
    "default-area-field",
    "default-area",
    "dry-run",
    "client-id",
    "app-id",
    "pull-request-events",
    "egress-policy",
    "allowed-endpoints",
    "extra-allowed-endpoints",
    "doppler-project",
    "doppler-config",
    "doppler-identity-id",
    "doppler-trusted-refs-only",
    "timeout-minutes",
}
# The inputs of safo's action.yml this wrapper may pass, frozen here.
SAFO_INPUTS = {
    "mode",
    "board-file",
    "client-id",
    "app-id",
    "private-key",
    "app-owner",
    "repositories",
    "token",
    "allow-token-for-org",
    "dry-run",
    "python-version",
    "status-body-file",
    "status-state",
    "project-url",
    "status-field",
    "status-open-issue",
    "status-open-pr",
    "status-draft-pr",
    "status-done",
    "done-date-field",
    "default-area-field",
    "default-area",
}


def doc() -> dict:
    return yaml.safe_load(WORKFLOW.read_text())


def steps() -> list[dict]:
    return doc()["jobs"]["sync"]["steps"]


def safo_step() -> dict:
    return next(s for s in steps() if str(s.get("uses", "")).startswith("ChiefGyk3D/scrum-around-and-find-out@"))


def test_every_input_a_caller_passes_today_is_still_accepted():
    inputs = set(doc()[True]["workflow_call"]["inputs"])  # PyYAML reads the bare key `on` as True
    assert CALLER_INPUTS <= inputs, f"callers would break: {sorted(CALLER_INPUTS - inputs)}"


def test_safo_is_called_by_commit_with_a_version_comment():
    match = re.search(
        r"uses: ChiefGyk3D/scrum-around-and-find-out@([0-9a-f]{40}) # v0\.1\.0\n", WORKFLOW.read_text()
    )
    assert match and match[1] != "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef01234567"
    expected = (REPO / "tests" / "safo-release-commit.txt").read_text().strip()
    assert re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{40}", expected) and match[1] == expected


def test_nothing_but_safo_mints_a_token_or_holds_a_script():
    assert not any("create-github-app-token" in str(s.get("uses", "")) for s in steps())
    assert not any("run" in s and ("python3 -" in s["run"] or "PROJECT_SYNC_PY" in s["run"]) for s in steps())
    assert not (REPO / "scripts" / "project_sync.py").exists()


def test_the_step_passes_only_inputs_safos_action_declares():
    passed = set(safo_step()["with"])
    assert passed <= SAFO_INPUTS, f"safo's action has no input {sorted(passed - SAFO_INPUTS)}"


def test_every_caller_input_that_means_something_to_safo_reaches_it():
    with_ = safo_step()["with"]
    for name in (
        "project-url",
        "status-field",
        "status-open-issue",
        "status-open-pr",
        "status-draft-pr",
        "status-done",
        "done-date-field",
        "default-area-field",
        "default-area",
        "dry-run",
        "client-id",
        "app-id",
    ):
        assert with_[name] == "${{ inputs." + name + " }}", name


def test_the_mode_follows_the_event_and_reconcile_false_is_a_no_op_on_schedule():
    step = safo_step()
    assert "github.event_name == 'schedule' || github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch'" in step["with"]["mode"]
    assert "'reconcile' || 'sync'" in step["with"]["mode"]
    assert "inputs.reconcile" in step["if"] and "'schedule'" in step["if"]


def test_the_token_is_limited_to_the_calling_repository_and_the_key_comes_from_doppler():
    with_ = safo_step()["with"]
    assert with_["repositories"] == "${{ github.event.repository.name }}"
    assert with_["private-key"] == "${{ env.PROJECTS_APP_PRIVATE_KEY }}"
    assert any(str(s.get("uses", "")).startswith("dopplerhq/secrets-fetch-action@") for s in steps())


def test_harden_runner_runs_first_and_the_default_list_lets_safo_install_pyyaml():
    first = steps()[0]
    assert first["uses"].startswith("step-security/harden-runner@")
    default = doc()[True]["workflow_call"]["inputs"]["allowed-endpoints"]["default"].split()
    assert {"api.github.com:443", "api.doppler.com:443", "pypi.org:443", "files.pythonhosted.org:443"} <= set(default)
```

Each is a one-line edit; `grep` finds them all.

grep -rn 'project_sync\|inline_project_sync' --include='*.md' --include='*.yml' --include='*.py' . | grep -v wrapper
```

- [ ] **Step 10: Open the GYST pull request, wait for CI, maintainer merges**

```bash
git add -A
git commit -m "project-sync.yml: a thin wrapper around scrum-around-and-find-out v0.1.0" -m "Inputs unchanged for callers; the script, its inliner and its tests move to the safo repository. Adds that repository to the audited list." -m "Co-Authored-By: Claude <noreply@anthropic.com>"
git push -u origin project-sync-wrapper
gh pr create -R ChiefGyk3D/git-your-ship-together --title "project-sync.yml: thin wrapper around safo v0.1.0" --body "See docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-07-safo-v0.1.0.md, task T12, in scrum-around-and-find-out."

Expected: a pull request URL; `CI green` including `fixture project sync`.

- [ ] **Step 11: MAINTAINER: cut the next GYST release**

A new GYST tag (fetch tags first; the next number after the latest) so callers can bump their pin.

#### Part C: the callers (the maintainer, one pull request each)

The six suite callers keep their `with:` unchanged and bump only the pin to the new GYST tag. Dependabot proposes it after the cooldown; or run GYST's re-pin habit across every caller together (a GYST bump moves ci, security, release, sync and fuzz as one). Hammunition's tray and gps-tether still need their Doppler identities (Hammunition #367) for event-driven sync; the weekly `reconcile` covers them meanwhile. After two weekly reconciles are checked by hand, the dotfiles board tools (`board_reconcile.py`, `fields_dump.py`, `board_migrate.py`, `agents-status`) retire: `fields_dump.py` is obsolete because ids are resolved live every run, `board_migrate.py` was a one-off migration, and the rest is ported.

## Self-review

What follows is anything in this plan that is **not grounded in code or documents I read**, and what was and was not run.

**Historical validation before the pre-scan amendments.** The original T1a–T11 blocks were executed in a scratch repository with tests first and `make check` green. The revised downstream counts start from the built 161-test baseline and end at 739 passed (513 before the 2026-10-08 hooks amendment); these counts include parametrized cases and the final removal of the wiki skip. The amended blocks must pass the task gates during implementation. `actionlint`, `zizmor --offline`, `shellcheck` and `shfmt` were run on the workflows and the script. The GYST changes in T12 were applied to a scratch copy of GYST `main` (its whole suite passes with them). Python 3.11 was not available here, so the suite ran on 3.13 only; ruff and mypy target 3.11.

**Amendment verification.** The later-task code and tests were reconstructed under `/tmp`, with the built T1a–T3 code preserved. Python syntax, strict typing, and regression logic were checked there. The sandbox blocks loopback sockets, so fake GitHub/Ollama responses used an in-memory transport shim for the regression checks; this is not a substitute for the ordinary loopback gates at implementation time.

**Not run.** Every step marked "not run": pushing, opening pull requests, `make venv` against the real hash locks (the locks were generated, and installed from, in the scratch venv), `scripts/apply-baseline.sh --apply`, the live audit, bootstrap and reconcile against real boards, tagging, and the GYST pull request's CI.

**Taken from memory or from the spec's lessons, not verified against GitHub, Codex, Claude Code, Copilot or Ollama.** The fakes model these, so a mistake here shows up on the first real run:

- GraphQL shapes: `createProjectV2Field` needing a colour and a description on every option, the `iterationConfiguration` input, `createProjectV2View`/`updateProjectV2View` and its `filter` (taken from the spec's lesson, not from the schema), `createProjectV2StatusUpdate`, `repositoryOwner`-free `organization`/`user` roots, `fieldValueByName` with `@include`, `items.totalCount`, and NOT_FOUND being the error type for a deleted node and for a repository the installation does not cover.
- Rate-limit detection: a 403 or 429 with `Retry-After`, or a body mentioning a secondary rate limit, or `x-ratelimit-remaining: 0`; anything else is a refusal.
- Codex: only `rate_limits` (`plan_type`, `primary`, `secondary`, `used_percent`, `resets_at`) is grounded (the prior-art `agents-status` reads it); `info.total_token_usage` and its key names are from memory.
- Claude Code transcripts: the row shape (`message.usage`, `message.id`, one row per content block), subagent transcripts living under the same `projects` tree, and the timestamp field are from memory.
- Copilot: that `gh agent-task list -L 200` prints an ISO date on each line (otherwise the count is reported as inexact), and the billing endpoint `/users/{login}/settings/billing/premium_request/usage` with `usageItems[].grossQuantity`. The `monthly_ai_credits: 7000` allowance in `agents.yaml` is the maintainer's measured quota, but the billing endpoint reports premium requests, so it is never divided by the AI-credit allowance. Routing's monthly threshold uses an explicit current-month meter with validated product, SKU and `ai_credits` units; unknown or incompatible data remains unknown.
- Ollama: `/api/tags`, `/api/ps` (`models[].name`) and a non-streaming `/api/generate` with a `think` flag and `eval_count`/`eval_duration` in the answer.
- Claude Code hooks: the SessionStart output (`hookSpecificOutput.additionalContext`), the PreToolUse input (`tool_name` Agent, or the older Task, with `tool_input.prompt`, `model` and `subagent_type`), the output that denies (`permissionDecision: deny` with `permissionDecisionReason`) or warns (`systemMessage`, `additionalContext`), the `settings.json` shape (`hooks.<Event>[]` groups with an optional `matcher` and `hooks[]` entries with `type`, `command`, `timeout`, `statusMessage`) and a fork having no `model` are taken from the maintainer's working prototypes and settings, not checked against current Claude Code documentation. The tests feed fixture payloads, so the first real session is the check.
- The reviewers I assigned to shapes the coordinator did not specify (research, plan-writing, integration, well-specified code, adversarial review, the local shapes) and the Codex weekly threshold of 90% are my choices; the stated routes and the 80% and 70% thresholds are as given. The 2026-10-07 evening amendment moved the routing data to the maintainer's measured order; the shapes it added or changed (well-specified-code now Haiku first, security-sensitive-code, ops-runbook), the reviewers of those shapes, the pins of the short-text local shapes to the 4B model, the `guard` role, `loaded_num_ctx`, `on_demand_models` and `keep_alive` schema additions that carry the exact-context rule, and the fall-back-with-a-warning for a missing pinned model are my reading of it. The 2048 `num_ctx` for the on-demand embedding model is a placeholder, not a measurement.

**Decisions that go beyond the spec's sketch** (each is optional in `board.yaml`, documented in `docs/board-yaml.md`): `rules.status.opened_pr` and `draft_pr` (GYST's separate pull-request and draft statuses), `rules.new_item_defaults`, `rules.add_closed_days` (the GYST project board's closed-in-30-days items), `repositories[].area_rules` (the title-keyword Area table in `board_reconcile.py`), an `agents:` section, an optional `project.number` (so `bootstrap` can create the project), and the Action accepting GYST's board-less inputs and both `client-id` and the deprecated `app-id`. When drift and an unanswerable question occur together, audit exits 2.

**Behaviour deliberately not ported.** `board_reconcile.py` flipped an open card in Done back to Backlog and classified Kind and Priority by title keywords; reconcile never reopens (GYST's rule) and audit reports it, and only a generic default (`new_item_defaults`) is kept. `fields_dump.py` is obsolete because ids are resolved live on every run, and `board_migrate.py` was a one-off migration, so neither is ported. Nothing GYST's `project_sync.py` did is lost: draft pull requests, `ready_for_review`/`converted_to_draft`, clearing the date on reopen, offline validation for credentialless CI (live dry run without a token exits 2), the user-project refusal (now credential-based), one-request item lookup, validation before any mutation, and `reconcile: false`. Its per-repository reconcile scope is replaced by `repositories` (the wrapper builds a one-repository board from the calling repository).

**Statements to check.** `docs/usage.md` says `safo usage` sends nothing about sessions anywhere and names its two network paths (`gh`'s own calls, and probes of your own endpoints); "nothing leaves the machine" is not literally true for the Copilot read, so it does not say that. The privacy test cannot contain the employer's name, a callsign or regions, so those come from a file outside the repository. `docs/superpowers/plans/` and `docs/superpowers/specs/` are excluded from the privacy scan. `docs/lessons.md` dates the first-week lessons as a range because the notes give one range, not a date each. The GYST wrapper's `allowed-endpoints` default (PyPI added) is a first guess to be corrected from the CI fixture's egress report. SAFO's release uses `python-package-release.yml` at v1.14.0, which predates GYST's SBOM inputs, so v0.1.0 ships without an SBOM. The PR grouping and the T-numbering suffixes (T1a to T1c, T9a to T9h, T10a to T10c) are my splits of the requested tasks. The Opus attribution line is on the spec and plan commits as asked; the plan's own commit steps use a generic `Claude` line.

## Amendment-review corrections (2026-10-07)

B1 restores sync's no-op exit 0. B2 delegates mutations to the built Client, preserves its bounded pre-execution
rate-limit waits, and retains every unknown-outcome regression. B3 excludes the spec tree alongside the plans.
M1 pins separate duplicate, alias, anchor, merge, depth and size refusals for base and override agents files.
M2 uses a valid no-op JSON event padded past 1 MiB. M3/M4 re-derive every downstream count with the specs present
and correct the wiki output to 14 files. M5 removes session scratch paths and corrects misleading test names.
M6 transfers the signed-tag-verified SHA between repositories in the exported SAFO_RELEASE_COMMIT variable.
M7 ships one small general Ollama model at num_ctx 8192; the optional two-GPU layout and shape pins live in the
local example. M8 indexes the initial board walk, batches missing-content discovery, verifies each acknowledged
add once, rereads the board once after adds, and reports adopted projects honestly. M9 documents that status
cannot verify whether an unknown update landed.

In-memory checks extract T4–T11 in order over the unchanged built T1a–T3 source, with the repository's specs present.
The T10b/T10c/T11 downstream checks passed at the time (399 passed + 1 skipped; 414 passed + 1 skipped; 423 passed).
Alias, merge-key and depth check removal each makes both base/override loader cases fail. Reinstating the old
unbounded event read makes the valid oversize JSON case fail (exit 0 instead of 2). Ruff and strict mypy pass
on the extracted final tree; routing and wiki generators report their expected check output.
Those runs omit the 90 built GraphQL/network-guard cases that require real sockets; their cases remain collected,
not rerun by the in-memory transport. Final collection was 513 tests with no skips at T11; the hooks amendment and its follow-ups below make it 739. The planned full gate
is **739 passed**; socket-based make check remains required during implementation.

## Hooks amendment (2026-10-08)

The maintainer's ruling of 2026-10-07 (the routing hardenings are part of SAFO) adds `safo hooks` and two consumers of it, and
changes three earlier tasks. Every changed or new block was extracted into a scratch copy of the repository at the commit
before this amendment, applied in plan order from T4 through T11, and run: tests first for the new tasks (T9g step 3 and T9h
failed as written), the task's own `pytest` line, and the gate (`make check`: `ruff`, `mypy --strict`, `pytest`) at T7, T9b,
T9c, T9g, T9h and from T10a on, with `pytest` alone at the other tasks. That original extraction ended at 632; the table below includes the adversarial follow-up and its newly collected regression cases. One
thing the run found that this amendment did not cause: at T4 as written, `make check` exits 2 because `items.py` imports
`ApiError` before T6 uses it; the later tasks make it clean.

| Task | Change | New cases | Gate after the task |
|---|---|---|---|
| T5 | `values.py`: live values are parsed into errors that name the field | 19 | 225 |
| T6 | reconcile refuses to call the board current while items are unread | 2 | 258 |
| T7 | `status --post`: cards grouped and counted in code (`statusgroups.py`), a template headline, `--print`; the fake gains `StatusItems`; a short listing and a malformed number or date stop the run | 35 | 326 |
| T8, T9a | counts only | 0 | 363, 368 |
| T9b | `agents-status` gains the routing-guard counts; new `guardlog.py` | 15 | 388 |
| T9c | an optional `hooks:` section in `agents.yaml` (approval token, probe age, local-step markers, the not-applicable marker) and a sixth local shape, `status-headline` | 12 | 428 |
| T9d, T9e, T9f | counts only | 0 | 466, 489, 512 |
| T9g | code renders the count sentence; a reachable local model may append one validated clause (`headline.py`) | 16 | 537 |
| T9h | `safo hooks install, probe, guard, mode, status`; `LocalContext` and `main()` gain `stdin` | 127 | 664 |
| T10a to T11 | documentation only (the README mode table gains `hooks`) | 0 | 666, 715, 730, 739 |

Each rule of the guard was removed in turn and a named test failed (T9h Step 4 lists them); the same was done for the status
grouping, the headline check and the guard-log reader. The rule the amendment adds to the playbook is that code does the
grouping, counting and count-sentence rendering; a model may append only constrained validated prose (`docs/usage.md`,
`docs/lessons.md`). The status prototype this ports read its project id and status names from the code; here they come from
`board.yaml`. T9f and T9g already carry the exact-`num_ctx` rule (`loaded_num_ctx`, refused before a request) and a per-call
log (`local run` appends an outcome line with the model, `think` and `num_ctx`); neither needed a change.

Not done, on purpose: the status headline call is not written to the outcomes log (it is not a finished task); the guard
does not read the brief's description; and `hooks install` does not edit a settings file it cannot parse. The Codex adversarial review is addressed by the follow-up below; real-session compatibility remains an explicit gate.


### Adversarial follow-up (2026-10-08): codex-t9h-review findings

All twelve findings and the two mutation-audit gaps are addressed in the executable plan blocks. Fail-open is retained;
healthy allow and degraded allow are observably different. The table identifies the fix and regression fixture for every
finding; the preceding T9h mutation matrix records how to remove each contract and detect the regression.

| Finding | Fixed in | Regression evidence |
|---|---|---|
| 1 — false hook ownership, matcher/type and duplicates | T9h `command_signature`, `_ensure`, `merge_settings`; exact parsed launcher/flags + event/matcher/type; preserve unrelated, refuse ambiguity/launcher change, normalize and deduplicate | false ownership, ambiguous entries, duplicate fields, changed launcher tests |
| 2 — settings loss, no backup, predictable temp | T9b `file_lock`/`write_text`; T9h `install_settings`; exclusive unpredictable temp, retained 0600 backup, byte conflict checks before replace, shared lock, fsync and cleanup, resolved symlink/permissions | external edit before/during temp write, cooperating installers, held lock, backup/private modes, interruption, temp-symlink tests |
| 3 — unbounded/nonregular reads | T9b `bounded_read`, `mode_health`, `read_log`; T9c `_read_yaml`/local override; T9h settings/state/event readers | handle read-size assertions, oversized files, FIFO/device and agents-input tests |
| 4 — silent degradation in block mode | T9h `diagnostic`, `run_probe`, `guard`, `run_guard`; T9b health reporting; fixed warning + bounded enum diagnostic, stdout diagnostic if disk unavailable, local-only versus all-rule failure defined | garbage, broken config, corrupt mode, unexpected exception, probe write and log-failure tests assert exit 0 AND visibility/record; denial survives log failure |
| 5 — globally shared/start-time probe state | T9h canonical `state_key`/`state_path`, `save_state`, completion timestamp, `state_health`; T9c default 300s, configured age honoured, lazy bounded `reprobe`, once-per-session notice, explicit stale/unknown/clock policy | session/config isolation, completion, out-of-order result, failed replacement and clock tests |
| 6 — hostile timestamp overflow/status crash | T9b `finite_time`; T9h `read_state`, `state_health`, `local_reachable`, `run_status`; finite/range checks before arithmetic, unknown/future/stale reporting retains model/approval checks | exponent overflow, 400-digit integer, negative, boolean and future timestamp tests, distinct clock health tests |
| 7 — concurrent log rotation/append and partial tails | T9h `append_log`; T9b complete-record reader; shared bounded lock, 2048-byte record cap, 4 MiB files, one private archive, tail recovery/newline requirement, retained-history counts | four-process rotation, held lock, truncated tail, oversized/invalid record and missing-newline tests |
| 8 — privacy/output injection in labels and forged rows | T9h normalized models, fixed guard/probe prose and label-free state; T9b `valid_record`/reader validates full schema/enums/timestamp/control-free output vocabulary | sentinels in all ten output-bearing record fields, raw-model and probe-label tests |
| 9 — swapped/missing counts or arbitrary headline claims | T7 `headline_acceptable`, `render_body`; T9g `choose_headline`/prompt: code renders the count sentence; only exact approved clause may follow | distinct 1/2/3/4 mapping, swapped labels, omitted counts, spelled numbers, claims, controls/Markdown and tampered-render tests |
| 10 — multiline response and incomplete fallback | T9g `local_headline` validates the whole response and catches defined operational exceptions; programmer defects remain visible | approved first line plus extra line is rejected; OSError/TimeoutError/UnicodeError/ValueError fallback tests |
| 11 — unpinned `--post` unknown-outcome reread | T7 `test_post_unknown_outcome_rereads_and_never_retries`, `post_update` comment: metadata reread cannot verify update landing, manual existing-update check remains | ordered mutation→reread assertion, no replay, both reread success and failure |
| 12 — Agent/Task mismatch and unverified runtime | T9h exact `Agent|Task` matcher + Step 5 compatibility gate against official docs and pinned Claude Code 2.1.260; actual warn/deny/fork/timeout/session smoke acceptance required | installed-matcher test fails with old Agent-only matcher; live acceptance explicitly NOT RUN, compatibility UNVERIFIED until it passes |
| Audit — mixed-case briefs and permission preservation | T9h actual uppercase prompt and nondefault 0640 symlink/new-file 0600 assertions | removing lowercase normalization or hardcoding 0644 fails |

Verification for the first follow-up: task-ordered extraction collected 703 final cases (71 more than the first hooks
amendment; 542 additions over the 161-collected built baseline); the re-review follow-up below adds 36 for **739**.
Intermediate gates retain the one historical wiki skip; T11 removes it. Focused totals: T5 39 (`test_bootstrap.py` and
`test_values.py`), T6 33, T7 68, T9b 20, T9c 40, T9g headline/status 50 and local-run 9, T9h 127. The complete **739 passed**
total is the implementation gate target and the verified result of the extracted run. In-memory fake GitHub/Ollama
transport, regular-file/FIFO/device fixtures and real local child processes exercised the changed contracts. Ruff and
strict mypy passed on the extracted code. Eighteen targeted old-code/mutation checks each made a named regression fail,
covering all findings and the audit gaps. The scratch extraction aligned the pre-existing baseline script/workflow/test
GYST pin with the plan's v1.15.0 pin; no repository file other than this plan was edited. Socket-based `make check` and the
pinned disposable Claude Code real-session gate remain required during implementation.


### Re-review follow-up (2026-10-08): `plan-t9h-rereview.md` N1, N3 to N6, and the T4 review's findings 1 and 2

The re-review ran the promised gate and found that `make check` was not green from T7 on. This pass re-extracted T4 through
T11 in plan order into a scratch copy of the built baseline and ran `make check` (ruff, `ruff format --check`,
`mypy --strict` over `src` and `tests`, pytest) at every task. Results: `ruff` clean from T6 (T4 and T5 keep the one
documented F401, `items.py` importing `ApiError` before T6 uses it), format clean, `mypy` clean at every task, pytest
**exit 0 at every task**: T7 326 passed + 1 skipped, T9h 664 + 1, T10c 730 + 1, T11 739. As in the first amendment the
scratch baseline's workflow and script pins were aligned to the plan's GYST v1.15.0 pin (the built repository already
carries it), otherwise `test_one_gyst_pin_in_every_caller` fails at T10c (N2 of the re-review).

| Finding | Fix | Regression evidence |
|---|---|---|
| N1 (blocking) — `mypy --strict` over tests | T7 `test_status.py` patches `"safo.modes.status.load_live"` by dotted name with a typed `reread(client, project) -> LiveBoard`; T9h tests use `os`, `time` and `safo.hooks.merge_settings` from their defining modules, the typed `Handle`, a `read_state` result checked for `None`, and a real `Endpoint` and `Probe` instead of `SimpleNamespace`; no `type: ignore`, no new `Any` | the extracted run: `mypy` exit 0 at T7, T9h, T10c, T11 |
| N3 — conflict check not pinned | the final comparison inside `write_text(before_replace=...)` has its own test: an edit that lands after the early comparison and before the replace | `test_the_final_conflict_check_runs_just_before_replacement` fails with only that argument removed |
| N4 — stale state turned the local-step rule off | `state_health` honours the configured age (no 300 s clamp); the guard calls `reprobe` for a stale state: at most one attempt a minute per session and configuration, 1.5 s per request, guard hook timeout 10 s, answer saved under the same key; a failed or rate-limited re-probe leaves reachability unknown (model and approval rules unchanged) and `first_notice` shows the degraded warning once per session while every dispatch is still logged | `test_a_stale_state_is_reprobed_once_and_the_fresh_answer_is_used`, `test_reprobe_is_rate_limited_and_a_failed_one_is_unknown_with_one_notice`, `test_a_configured_age_above_five_minutes_is_honoured`; the other stale-state tests set `block_reprobe` so the state stays stale |
| N5 — venv or pipx launcher | `--command` also accepts one absolute, normalized path ending in `safo`, which must be an existing regular executable file (a pipx symlink resolves to one); ownership of an installed hook recognizes the same shape | `test_install_accepts_the_absolute_path_of_a_safo_executable`; six refusals in `test_install_refuses_a_launcher_path_that_is_not_an_executable_safo` |
| N6 — backup left after an aborted replace | a detected conflict removes the backup it made (a failed write still keeps it) | both conflict tests assert no `.settings.json.backup-*` remains |
| T4 review 1 — a lagging listing reported clean | T6 `reconcile` counts the items it actually read (listed, or looked up by content id) against `live.items_total`; fewer is `UNKNOWN`, exit 2, never "nothing to do"; T7 `status --post` raises `ApiError` for a short listing instead of writing an update that misses cards. T5 bootstrap and T7 sync never decide from a listing (sync looks one card up by id) | `test_a_listing_that_hides_cards_nobody_looked_up_is_never_reported_current` (with a visible control), `test_a_short_board_listing_stops_the_built_update` |
| T4 review 2 — malformed live values raised bare `ValueError` | new T5 `values.py` (`whole_number`, `iso_day`) raises `ApiError` naming the field and never echoing the value; bootstrap's created and discovered project numbers and status's card numbers use it, and status's `--start-date` and `--target-date` raise `ConfigError` like `--since` | `tests/test_values.py` (18 cases), the bootstrap, status number and status date tests |

Each new test was shown red by removing only its fix in a copy of the extracted tree (15 mutations, each red, control
green first). Limits: the T4 section of this plan still carries `live.py`'s own `int()` and `fromisoformat` calls and
`audit.py`'s lag check; the coordinator's in-code T4 fix owns them, and the later tasks do not depend on it. Reconcile
may still apply its idempotent writes to the work it knows about before it reports the unread items, so a short listing
cannot yield a success exit but does not block those writes. Whether GitHub's `items.totalCount` includes archived items
is unmeasured; the real-board dogfood in T11 is the check.
